Pairs (delta = first - second; + = the first costs):

- D-F = DEFAULT - FULL: R-14 verify (trim + pad) vs R-13's whole-run verify
- N-F = NOPAD - FULL: r14b's trim alone vs R-13's whole-run verify
- D-N = DEFAULT - NOPAD: r14p's padded word on top of the trim
- D-K = DEFAULT - KBDENY: KB, trim + pad on in both (slot20's KB question on R-14's verify)
- F-K = FULL - KBDENY: R-13's verify with KA+KB vs KA alone with trim + pad on (approximates slot20's D-K)
- F-FK = FULL - FULLKB: R-13's verify with KA+KB vs KA alone, both whole-run (slot20's D-K, text for text)
- ON-OFF = DEFAULT - OFF: SIMD on vs the scalar layer
